# Household hh_s9 — 28 days, Monday to Sunday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: couple_plus_one. Rooms: bathroom, bedroom_1, bedroom_2, entry, kitchen, living, office, storage.

Residents:

- **Ines** (resident_1): retired; fairly tidy (tidiness 0.61); average timing (jitter scale 1.17); rarely forgets pocket items; mood barely affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.29); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: friends, music, plants. Chores they take on: dusting, fixing, grocery_run.
- **Yuki** (resident_2): retired; very tidy (tidiness 0.94); average timing (jitter scale 1.04); often forgets pocket items; mood barely affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.36); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: meditation, movies, plants. Chores they take on: dusting, grocery_run, vacuuming.
- **Hana** (resident_3): works from home; very tidy (tidiness 0.76); punctual (jitter scale 0.56); rarely forgets pocket items; mood strongly affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.94); sleeps in bedroom_2, works at the office desk. Hobbies: movies, snacking. Chores they take on: dusting, fixing, grocery_run.

Pet: a dog, mainly looked after by Yuki.

## Day 5 — Saturday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d5`: Yuki hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:30–16:50).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_1:d5`: Ines gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (20:38–21:23).
- `ep:sore_back:resident_2:d5`: Yuki has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).
- `running_late:resident_2`: Yuki is running late all day (0.73): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

Internal states (0–1): Ines energy 0.37, hurriedness 0.62, distraction 0.50; Yuki energy 0.98, hurriedness 0.73, distraction 0.44; Hana energy 0.46, hurriedness 0.55, distraction 0.39

## Day 14 — Monday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d14`: Yuki hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:35–16:08).
- `ep:deadline:resident_3:d14:looming`: Hana has a deadline tomorrow at noon: works into the evening, eats fast with the laptop at the table (18:00–24:00).
- `ep:early_night:resident_3:d14`: Hana turns in early tonight: a short evening, bed 45 minutes sooner (20:30–24:00).
- `ep:sore_back:resident_2:d14`: Yuki has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).

## Day 15 — Tuesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_1:d15`: Ines hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:50–16:36).
- `ep:deadline:resident_3:d14:due_today`: Hana's deadline is at noon: a hurried morning, laptop and notebook never out of reach (06:30–12:00).
- `ep:deadline:resident_3:d14:relief`: Hana made the deadline and is running on empty: things get left out, and the evening is TV (12:00–24:00).

Internal states (0–1): Ines energy 0.49, hurriedness 0.52, distraction 0.62; Yuki energy 0.73, hurriedness 0.40, distraction 0.69; Hana energy 0.44, hurriedness 0.23, distraction 0.35

## Day 22 — Tuesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_1:d22`: Ines hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:08–15:51).
- `ep:deadline:resident_3:d22:looming`: Hana has a deadline tomorrow at noon: works into the evening, eats fast with the laptop at the table (18:00–24:00).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_3:d22`: Hana gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (20:59–21:44).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_1:d22`: Ines can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (20:24–24:00).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_3:d22`: Hana can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (20:57–24:00).

Internal states (0–1): Ines energy 0.41, hurriedness 0.59, distraction 0.56; Yuki energy 0.26, hurriedness 0.64, distraction 0.34; Hana energy 0.65, hurriedness 0.36, distraction 0.43

## Day 23 — Wednesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d23`: Ines is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:03–15:33).
- `ep:deadline:resident_3:d22:due_today`: Hana's deadline is at noon: a hurried morning, laptop and notebook never out of reach (06:30–12:00).
- `ep:deadline:resident_3:d22:relief`: Hana made the deadline and is running on empty: things get left out, and the evening is TV (12:00–24:00).
- `ep:early_night:resident_2:d23`: Yuki turns in early tonight: a short evening, bed 45 minutes sooner (20:30–24:00).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_1:d23`: Ines is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (10:55–12:55).

Internal states (0–1): Ines energy 0.40, hurriedness 0.40, distraction 0.59; Yuki energy 0.27, hurriedness 0.62, distraction 0.52; Hana energy 0.83, hurriedness 0.15, distraction 0.33
